      "mode": "direct",
      "pilot_admission": "all four terminal pilot scopes; ordinary mistakes retained",
      "policy": "i01-assisted-feasibility-2026-10-16",
      "prompt_system": {
        "content": "You are one of two agents finding the global maximum across your current\nprivate integer shards. Both agents must separately submit that same GLOBAL\nmaximum. Submitting only your own local maximum is insufficient when the\nother shard contains a larger value.\n\nYour CURRENT AUTHORITATIVE PRIVATE SHARD is your source for current local\nvalues. The other agent's shard is private. You can exchange ordinary text\nmessages. A message is a claim, not a certified source or a private submission.\nThe partner-finished flag reveals only whether that agent has finished.\n\nA useful strategy is to establish the relevant maximum of each shard, compare\nthem, and send your partner any information needed before you finish. Choose\nyour messages and timing. Reuse earlier reports when justified; obtain new\ninformation when needed. You do not have to repeat an exchange merely because\na new phase starts. Check what a source change invalidates and what remains\nuseful. An earlier final answer is not automatically a current answer.\n\nAn ENVIRONMENT SOURCE NOTICE is not a partner message, a partner maximum or\na partner submission. Its record index identifies a location, not an answer.\nAt a source-change boundary only the named record changes. The replacement\nvalue is supplied only to that record's owner. Earlier-phase history records\nwhat was visible then; current values come from your current shard.\n\nReturn exactly one JSON object with exactly these keys:\nmessage: a string containing non-whitespace text or null\nsubmit: an integer or null\nwait: true or false\n\nLegal choices are message only, submit only, message and submit together, or\nwait alone. Waiting requires BOTH message and submit to be null. With wait\nfalse, at least one of message or submit must be non-null.\nMessage-only example: {\"message\":\"example message\",\"submit\":null,\"wait\":false}\nWait-only example: {\"message\":null,\"submit\":null,\"wait\":true}\nThese are action-form examples, not messages to copy for the task.\n\nYour submission is private unless you explicitly message it. Submission is\nFINAL for this phase: you cannot adjust it after another message arrives.\nSending and submitting together delivers your message and then finishes you.\nOnly an environment source change starts a new phase and reopens both agents.\n\nEach agent has at most three calls per phase. Unused calls cannot be transferred.\nWaiting wakes only on a delivered message. If both agents wait, neither can\nsend a waking message. Invalid replies use a call and deliver nothing.\nThere is no correctness feedback. Put no text outside the JSON object.",
        "role": "system"
      },
      "settings": {
        "attention": "recovery_t4_sdpa",
        "chat_template": "{%- set image_count = namespace(value=0) %}\n{%- set video_count = namespace(value=0) %}\n{%- macro render_content(content, do_vision_count, is_system_content=false) %}\n    {%- if content is string %}\n        {{- content }}\n    {%- elif content is iterable and content is not mapping %}\n        {%- for item in content %}\n            {%- if 'image' in item or 'image_url' in item or item.type == 'image' %}\n                {%- if is_system_content %}\n                    {{- raise_exception('System message cannot contain images.') }}\n                {%- endif %}\n                {%- if do_vision_count %}\n                    {%- set image_count.value = image_count.value + 1 %}\n                {%- endif %}\n                {%- if add_vision_id %}\n                    {{- 'Picture ' ~ image_count.value ~ ': ' }}\n                {%- endif %}\n                {{- '<|vision_start|><|image_pad|><|vision_end|>' }}\n            {%- elif 'video' in item or item.type == 'video' %}\n                {%- if is_system_content %}\n                    {{- raise_exception('System message cannot contain videos.') }}\n                {%- endif %}\n                {%- if do_vision_count %}\n                    {%- set video_count.value = video_count.value + 1 %}\n                {%- endif %}\n                {%- if add_vision_id %}\n                    {{- 'Video ' ~ video_count.value ~ ': ' }}\n                {%- endif %}\n                {{- '<|vision_start|><|video_pad|><|vision_end|>' }}\n            {%- elif 'text' in item %}\n                {{-       "prior_spending": [],
      "prompt_system": {
        "content": "You are one of two agents finding the global maximum across your current\nprivate integer shards. Both agents must separately submit that same GLOBAL\nmaximum. Submitting only your own local maximum is insufficient when the\nother shard contains a larger value.\n\nYour CURRENT AUTHORITATIVE PRIVATE SHARD is your source for current local\nvalues. The other agent's shard is private. You can exchange ordinary text\nmessages. A message is a claim, not a certified source or a private submission.\nThe partner-finished flag reveals only whether that agent has finished.\n\nA useful strategy is to establish the relevant maximum of each shard, compare\nthem, and send your partner any information needed before you finish. Choose\nyour messages and timing. Reuse earlier reports when justified; obtain new\ninformation when needed. You do not have to repeat an exchange merely because\na new phase starts. Check what a source change invalidates and what remains\nuseful. An earlier final answer is not automatically a current answer.\n\nAn ENVIRONMENT SOURCE NOTICE is not a partner message, a partner maximum or\na partner submission. Its record index identifies a location, not an answer.\nAt a source-change boundary only the named record changes. The replacement\nvalue is supplied only to that record's owner. Earlier-phase history records\nwhat was visible then; current values come from your current shard.\n\nReturn exactly one JSON object with exactly these keys:\nmessage: a string containing non-whitespace text or null\nsubmit: an integer or null\nwait: true or false\n\nLegal choices are message only, submit only, message and submit together, or\nwait alone. Waiting requires BOTH message and submit to be null. With wait\nfalse, at least one of message or submit must be non-null.\nMessage-only example: {\"message\":\"example message\",\"submit\":null,\"wait\":false}\nWait-only example: {\"message\":null,\"submit\":null,\"wait\":true}\nThese are action-form examples, not messages to copy for the task.\n\nYour submission is private unless you explicitly message it. Submission is\nFINAL for this phase: you cannot adjust it after another message arrives.\nSending and submitting together delivers your message and then finishes you.\nOnly an environment source change starts a new phase and reopens both agents.\n\nEach agent has at most three calls per phase. Unused calls cannot be transferred.\nWaiting wakes only on a delivered message. If both agents wait, neither can\nsend a waking message. Invalid replies use a call and deliver nothing.\nThere is no correctness feedback. Put no text outside the JSON object.",
        "role": "system"
      },

| retain | 2 | 0 | 0.1 |
| clear | 3 | 0 | 0.15 |

Stratum coverage/empirical variation: [{"owner": 0, "first": 0, "intended": 5, "known_pairs": 5, "retain_only": 0, "clear_only": 1, "empty_observed_stratum": false, "constant_observed_contrast": false}, {"owner": 0, "first": 1, "intended": 5, "known_pairs": 5, "retain_only": 1, "clear_only": 0, "empty_observed_stratum": false, "constant_observed_contrast": false}, {"owner": 1, "first": 0, "intended": 5, "known_pairs": 5, "retain_only": 1, "clear_only": 0, "empty_observed_stratum": false, "constant_observed_contrast": false}, {"owner": 1, "first": 1, "intended": 5, "known_pairs": 5, "retain_only": 0, "clear_only": 2, "empty_observed_stratum": false, "constant_observed_contrast": false}]. Constant or empty observed strata cannot reveal unobserved discordance.

## Exact main process observables

Calls are dependent and their counts depend on behavior. Physical attempt-level validity is in attempt_compliance.csv; logical registered-call validity is in protocol_compliance.csv. No independent-binomial or causal failure-percentage interpretation is selected.

| Phase | Terminal /20 | Both submitted | Agreement | Wrong agreement | Locally refutable /40 slots | Present submissions /40 | Unknown submission slots | Obsolete submissions |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| prefix | 20 | 16 | 10 | 0 | 0 | 36 | 0 | 0 |
| retain | 20 | 8 | 3 | 1 | 3 | 22 | 0 | 0 |
| clear | 20 | 10 | 4 | 1 | 2 | 29 | 0 | 0 |

Clear lacks the withdrawn unique value through its current input and erased history. Old-answer repetition alone does not identify anchoring or successful correction. Correct-prefix results are descriptive counts only; no subgroup interval/test is supplied. Unknown effort stays unknown; observed sums have their own recorded coverage.

## Deterministically selected casebook

One lowest-index main world per category; overlaps are allowed. Fixed phase/event order determines call references. Absent means no observed qualifying case in the recorded main evidence; unknown coverage remains visible. Human descriptions/reviewer identity remain pending unless actually supplied.

- illegal_action: main-03; selected call main-03/clear/agent-0/invocation-0; human review pending.
- locally_refutable_recovery_submission: main-03; selected call main-03/retain/agent-0/invocation-1; human review pending.
- retained_obsolete_answer: absent among recorded main traces; selected call none; human review pending.
- clear_only_success: main-00; selected call main-00/prefix/agent-0/invocation-0; human review pending.
- retain_only_success: main-01; selected call main-01/prefix/agent-1/invocation-0; human review pending.
- both_success: absent among recorded main traces; selected call none; human review pending.

Observed cohort contrast is exact when complete; exploratory bootstrap resamples worlds, not repeated generations or all model seeds.
